SAW MILL RIVER AT YONKERS NY, USGS site 01376500 (USGS-01376500), stream in Westchester County, New York, draining 25.6 square miles. Daily means from 1943-11-10 to 1995-09-29, a page a water year (October to September), linked below. Discharge in cubic feet a second, gage height in feet above the gauge's datum. Its annual peaks are its floods; its field measurements are the visits that check the gauge. Revisions are USGS's own notes of the site's published history, as written.
